Size buckets are line counts of the whole file: `s` < 300 · `m` 300–799 · `L` 800–1499 · `XL` ≥ 1500.

`pub(crate)` / `pub(super)` items are counted under `crate-vis`; `pub use` under `re-export`. Notable types are `pub` structs, enums and traits.
